# AI Media Scout — 2026-10-30

Today’s expanded edition offers 49 detailed guides, including 20 completed from earlier screened entries, and 31 additional newly screened tools. It adds 60 distinct tools to the cumulative library. Coverage spans 75 fields, 623 configured repository queries and 18 model tasks; archived search failures, partial results and bounded pages remain disclosed below. Live primary-source research supplements every field, including neural audio patching and learned light shaping. All platforms were searched equally; individual tools retain their documented platform limits. The two new GitLab guides distinguish software grants from model/service costs. None of these applications was installed or run by AI Media Scout, and no new entry earned Recommended or Ready to try without independent evidence. First profile means new to this library, not necessarily launched today. All repository queries returned successful attempts, with 122 bounded searches. One text-to-image model-task response exceeded the collector’s 2 MB archival limit; web research supplements that explicit gap. Codeberg and SourceHut access gaps are recorded separately.

Documentation reviewed today. Tools are not hands-on tested unless explicitly stated. Requirements and performance remain source-specific.

## Coverage

| Field | Finding |
| --- | --- |
| Images & design | Primary image-canvas documentation was researched, including Invoke and Moka. Browser/native interfaces do not establish local inference support; model and hardware terms remain separate. Moka’s project page had no extractable text in this web read. [Source 1](https://invoke.ai/) [Source 2](https://moka-canvas.art/) |
| Video, animation & film | OpenReelio’s primary site describes an inspectable AI command log with a multitrack editor. New editing workspaces were searched broadly; polished interface claims do not establish reliable automated edits. [Source 1](https://openreelio.com/) |
| Audio, music & voice | Local neural music studios and synthesis were researched. OpenSuno’s primary site distinguishes MIT application code from non-commercial YuE2 and associated weights; Windows support remains experimental. [Source 1](https://opensuno.org/) |
| 3D, reconstruction & assets | Parametric modeling and AI-agent CAD were researched through SolidCraft and SOMA. Editable geometry/export capability is distinct from verified dimensional accuracy or fabrication readiness. [Source 1](https://solidcraft.io/) [Source 2](https://somacad.app/) |
| AI audio patching and musical dataflow | The expanded search found a learned Max for Live performance mixer and an agent-driven OSC/MIDI integration. Both are screened leads with specific setup/documentation discrepancies, rather than qualified performance recommendations. RAVE and nn_tilde remain excluded because their reviewed software grants are non-commercial. The primary RAVE paper was revisited for learned audio synthesis. IRCAM’s project page returned mainly navigation in the text view; neither that partial view nor the paper changes the reviewed non-commercial software grant. [Source 1](https://github.com/mkomorny/dub-assistant-neural-mixer) [Source 2](https://github.com/roomi-fields/osc-bridge-skill) [Source 3](https://github.com/acids-ircam/RAVE/blob/master/LICENSE) [Source 4](https://github.com/acids-ircam/nn_tilde/blob/master/LICENSE) [Source 5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2111.05011) [Source 6](https://forum.ircam.fr/projects/detail/rave/) |
| Learned light shaping and caustic art | Learned caustic-image generation and neural shape-from-caustics research were examined as light-art adjacencies. The web research did not establish a newly eligible, licensed creator application. Classical caustic optimization alone is not evidence of a neural tool. [Source 1](https://opg.optica.org/ao/abstract.cfm?uri=ao-65-10-D69) [Source 2](https://diglib.eg.org/bitstream/handle/10.2312/vmv20231224/033-041.pdf) |
| Browser tools & web media | Browser AI and creative-canvas sources were researched. WebLLM provides an in-browser model runtime, whereas ordinary WebGPU effects are not automatically AI tools. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15803) |
| WebXR, VR & AR | Meta’s primary IWSDK announcement describes agent-written and tested immersive-web scenes. This establishes an AI-assisted authoring route, not universal headset support or independently verified experience quality. [Source 1](https://developers.meta.com/vr/blog/accelerate-vr-development-with-ai-and-immersive-web-sdk/) |
| Computational art & creative coding | Creative-coding environments and neural/agent authoring were researched. Fragment’s ordinary shader and MIDI tools are not counted as AI simply because an agent could write code for them. [Source 1](https://fragment.tools/) |
| Interactive, immersive & live media | Primary installation software documentation includes ONNX pose detection controlling media and lights. Interaction support remains distinct from reliable unattended operation. [Source 1](https://ossia.io/interactive-installations.html) |
| 3D printing & generative CAD | SOMA’s primary documentation covers AI-authored editable CadQuery models and physical-design examples. A STEP/STL export is not a guarantee of printability, load capacity or manufactured accuracy. [Source 1](https://somacad.app/) |
| Games & production pipelines | ForgeAX and Evoke broadened the game search from agent-authored editable code to learned interactive worlds. Their architecture and author demos do not establish universal game-production readiness. [Source 1](https://forgeax.github.io/docs/) |
| Motion capture & character animation | Movens-Zero’s primary project documents recovered and generated motion from several modalities. Research/model availability alone does not establish an artist-ready animation application. [Source 1](https://movens-zero.github.io/) |
| Avatars, digital humans & lip sync | AVTR-1’s primary model card links the inference code and specifies CUDA/TensorRT and Ampere-or-newer GPU prerequisites. Weight availability and an avatar demo do not settle all software/asset terms. [Source 1](https://huggingface.co/avaturn-live/avtr-1/blob/main/README.md) |
| VFX, compositing & relighting | Light-aware compositing and BlenderFusion research supplemented keying/compositing discovery. A paper or conventional compositor alone is not a newly reviewed open-source AI application. [Source 1](https://yaksoy.github.io/papers/SIG25p-LightAwareCompositing.pdf) |
| Spatial audio & volumetric media | Neural acoustic-field research was examined for immersive spatial sound. Conventional room simulation is not classified as neural inference without concrete evidence. [Source 1](https://seexpeng.github.io/data/papers/Neural%20Acoustic%20Fields%20for%20Mixed%20Reality.pdf) |
| Photogrammetry, scanning & neural rendering | Primary volumetric-capture research describes RGB/RGB-D reconstruction into point clouds and Gaussian splats. Capture quality, synchronization and usable source licensing require separate workflow checks. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15719) |
| Editing, captions & post-production | Subtitle Edit’s current primary documentation lists local/cloud speech models and AI review alongside manual subtitle tools. Recognition, translation, timing and provider privacy remain separate checks. [Source 1](https://subtitleedit.github.io/subtitleedit/overview.html) |
| Vector graphics, illustration & textures | SVGRender and StarVector primary sources cover learned raster-to-vector and text/image-to-SVG workflows. Editable output is not proof of geometric cleanliness or design quality. [Source 1](https://huggingface.co/SVGRender) |
| Typography, fonts & layout | VecGlypher research generates editable vector glyphs with image/text conditioning. Source availability, font rights, script coverage and operator setup were not established merely from the paper. [Source 1](https://xk-huang.github.io/VecGlypher/assets/VecGlypher.pdf) |
| Storyboarding, narrative & comics | Primary storyboard/comic project sites were researched. Dramai’s own page says its v0.1 ships storyboard generation while later pipeline stages remain planned, so marketing-stage diagrams are not treated as completed functionality. [Source 1](https://hyyyyyyz.github.io/dramai/) |
| Creative publishing & presentation | OpenSlides and Amaroad primary sites describe agent-editable decks, live previews and export routes. AI provider terms and the fidelity of static PowerPoint export remain distinct from the app’s software license. [Source 1](https://openslides.dev/) |
| Photography, restoration & color | Primary research on learned restoration of historical Autochrome defects was checked. Restored appearance is not evidence that inferred historical detail is authentic. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22291) |

## Search and review counts

75 creative fields; 623/623 repository queries attempted; 18/18 model-task queries attempted. 28237 distinct source candidates and 810 model leads. 49 detailed profiles and 31 additional screened discoveries. Source gaps: 0 failed repository queries, 0 partial repository queries, 122 bounded repository queries, 1 failed model-task queries and 2 web ecosystem gaps. Raw search candidates include duplicates of known tools, excluded projects and projects awaiting review; they are not verified recommendations.

## Research beyond GitHub

- **gitlab** · searched: OpenDub and OpenSlides were researched through live primary sites and the GitLab API. Complete software grants, README snapshots and bounded maintenance records were archived; both received full guides. This resolves yesterday’s OpenDub license-access gap without treating every optional model as cleared. [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub) [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides) [Source](https://openslides.dev/)
- **codeberg** · gap: A live ecosystem search was blocked by robots restrictions. No newly eligible source/license result was verified through that access route; this is a coverage gap, not absence of relevant software.
- **sourcehut** · gap: Live search access was blocked by robots restrictions. The dated failed attempt is archived; no completeness claim is made for this ecosystem.
- **packages** · searched: DFlat’s dflat-opt package page documents neural optical-response surrogates and differentiable rendering. Its description says current MIT while package metadata says GPLv3; package labels alone were not accepted as a complete software-license review. The unrelated older dflat preservation package was distinguished. [Source](https://pypi.org/project/dflat-opt/) [Source](https://pypi.org/project/dflat/)
- **creative-plugins** · searched: Primary ComfyUI custom-node documentation was reviewed for PromptForge, SpEdE and new model/media integrations. A registry search did not yield an additional verified SpEdE listing. Core-node versions, gsplat/CUDA compatibility and model terms were kept separate from plugin-code licensing. [Source](https://github.com/wwapw29/ComfyUI-PromptForge) [Source](https://github.com/Burgstall-labs/ComfyUI-SpEdE)
- **project-sites** · searched: Live primary project sites supplemented all 75 observed fields, from creative editing and interactive installations to neural optics, haptics, scent and physical craft. A hosted free tool, author video or promised release does not establish an open-source software grant. [Source](https://openslides.dev/) [Source](https://ossia.io/interactive-installations.html) [Source](https://opencadirrigation.com/)
- **research-code** · searched: Primary papers/model pages covered neural acoustics, materials, motion, visual dubbing and generated worlds. Several project pages still say code coming soon; these were retained as research context rather than published as installable applications. [Source](https://midotronn.github.io/MAESTRO/) [Source](https://bowenxueai.github.io/VideoNeuMat/) [Source](https://naaapi.github.io/PHOSA/) [Source](https://huggingface.co/morphic/reshoot-anything/blob/main/README.md?code=true)
- **international** · searched: Chinese, Vietnamese, French, Russian/English and Persian/RTL creative workflows were examined in current primary documentation. LocalVideoFactory, French typography revision, Manga Voice Studio and MotionKit illustrate why language, platform, source setup and actual output evidence remain separate. [Source](https://github.com/hafung/LocalVideoFactory) [Source](https://github.com/painhardcore/le-unslop) [Source](https://github.com/Yurichz/manga-voice-studio) [Source](https://github.com/immousavi/motionkit) [Source](https://github.com/Aladinvietnam/ke-truyen)

## Dralo Studio

Video, animation & film · Storyboarding, narrative & comics · Editing, captions & post-production · Browser tools & web media

First complete guide in this library, including completion of any earlier screened entry; not a claim of a launch today.

### Quality assessment: Experimental

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Developing a short film or motion comic from a script while keeping local project data, assets and shot revisions together.

Evidence checked: 2026-10-30. No local execution implied.

Why Experimental: The current R3 standalone source release is explicitly a beta; model and platform validation is incomplete, and older database migrations have restricted upgrade paths. https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/Dralo-Studio/blob/main/README.md https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/docs/QUICKSTART.md https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/Dralo-Studio/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · unknown:** The README explicitly labels its workflow graphics as diagrams rather than screenshots or tested generated films. They illustrate organization, not output-quality evidence. [Source](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/Dralo-Studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/docs/QUICKSTART.md) [Source](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Prepare Node 22+, pnpm 11.x, Python 3.12 and FFmpeg/FFprobe. Create the virtual environment, install the locked Python dependencies, initialize local configuration, install both frontend dependency trees, build, then initialize the database and run the API and worker in separate terminals. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/Dralo-Studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/docs/QUICKSTART.md) [Source](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** The bounded latest-release query returned v0.1.0-preview.3 (2026-10-19T14:01:37Z). The latest-updated query returned 0 issue/pull-request records, with a maximum of 10. This snapshot and the reviewed setup/limitations do not establish sustained support or all blocker resolutions. [Source](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/Dralo-Studio/releases) [Source](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/Dralo-Studio/issues)
- **Independent use · unknown:** No substantive independent first-hand use was established in the reviewed primary documentation and bounded issue activity. Owner reports, PR descriptions and promotional comments do not independently validate this workflow. [Source](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/Dralo-Studio/issues) [Source](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/Dralo-Studio/blob/main/README.md)
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** Apache-2.0 original code; integrated MIT components retain notices. Model providers, cloud object storage, user-supplied character assets and generation costs have independent terms. No model weights, provider credits or unlicensed character packs are included. Local compute plus any chosen external model or service; provider access and charges are separate. [Source](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/Dralo-Studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/docs/QUICKSTART.md) [Source](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. R3 is a standalone source beta. Old or unknown databases require the published upgrade procedure, not direct replacement. Local storage does not mean model inference stays offline; some providers need publicly reachable reference-asset storage.

### How it uses AI

A standalone short-drama workspace connects AI-assisted scripts, reusable characters, storyboards, image/video generation and a multitrack timeline through user-configured model providers. [Source](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/Dralo-Studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/docs/QUICKSTART.md) [Source](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md)

### Introduction

A standalone short-drama workspace connects AI-assisted scripts, reusable characters, storyboards, image/video generation and a multitrack timeline through user-configured model providers. Developing a short film or motion comic from a script while keeping local project data, assets and shot revisions together. ### Install

Prepare Node 22+, pnpm 11.x, Python 3.12 and FFmpeg/FFprobe. Create the virtual environment, install the locked Python dependencies, initialize local configuration, install both frontend dependency trees, build, then initialize the database and run the API and worker in separate terminals. [Source 1](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/Dralo-Studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/docs/QUICKSTART.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Prepare Node 22+, pnpm 11.x, Python 3.12 and FFmpeg/FFprobe. Create the virtual environment, install the locked Python dependencies, initialize local configuration, install both frontend dependency trees, build, then initialize the database and run the API and worker in separate terminals.

```sh
python -m venv .venv
```


```sh
python -m pip install --only-binary=:all: -r apps/server/requirements.lock.txt
```


```sh
python scripts/init_local_config.py
```


```sh
pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
```


```sh
pnpm --dir apps/director-upstream install --frozen-lockfile
```


```sh
pnpm build
```


```sh
cd apps/server
```


```sh
python -m app.standalone_initialize
```


```sh
python -m uvicorn app.main:app --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8000
```


```sh
python -m app.jobs.worker
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/Dralo-Studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/docs/QUICKSTART.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md)

1. Activate the same Python environment separately for the API and worker, then open the local page and sign in using the locally initialized account.
2. Add your own model channel, import a short script and organize characters and shots.
3. Generate one reviewed shot before assembling media and subtitles on the timeline; inspect output and provider usage.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/Dralo-Studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/docs/QUICKSTART.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Node.js 22+, repository-pinned pnpm 11.x, Python 3.12, FastAPI/SQLite and FFmpeg/FFprobe. The source guide separately installs the 3D director’s frontend dependencies.
- **Platforms:** The first public source install was checked on Windows/Python 3.12.14. macOS/Linux instructions exist but do not establish completed platform validation. No standalone Windows/macOS installer is supplied.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/Dralo-Studio/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/Dralo-Studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/docs/QUICKSTART.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md)

- **Code:** Apache-2.0
- **Weights:** Apache-2.0 original code; integrated MIT components retain notices. Model providers, cloud object storage, user-supplied character assets and generation costs have independent terms. No model weights, provider credits or unlicensed character packs are included.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows Apache-2.0. Apache-2.0 original code; integrated MIT components retain notices. Model providers, cloud object storage, user-supplied character assets and generation costs have independent terms. No model weights, provider credits or unlicensed character packs are included.
- **Cost:** Local compute plus any chosen external model or service; provider access and charges are separate.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/Dralo-Studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/docs/QUICKSTART.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md)

### Limitations

R3 is a standalone source beta. Old or unknown databases require the published upgrade procedure, not direct replacement. Local storage does not mean model inference stays offline; some providers need publicly reachable reference-asset storage. [Source 1](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/Dralo-Studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/docs/QUICKSTART.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/lnblfkj-del/dralo-studio/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md)

First complete guide in this library, including completion of any earlier screened entry; not a claim of a launch today.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Scope: Drafting connected rooms, autotiled maps and minimaps for later integration with Unity, Godot, Unreal or SDL projects.

Evidence checked: 2026-10-30. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The README provides editing GIFs and a six-room sample world. These demonstrate editor operations, not independently validated AI level-design quality. [Source](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/docs/mcp/setup.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Use a release launcher with ASP.NET Core Runtime 10, or build with Node 24+, .NET SDK 10 and Git. For agent control, install the CLI and configure a compatible MCP client to launch a version-pinned server against an existing workspace. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/docs/mcp/setup.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** The bounded latest-release query returned v1.3.2 (2026-09-09T03:59:22Z). The latest-updated query returned 0 issue/pull-request records, with a maximum of 10. This snapshot and the reviewed setup/limitations do not establish sustained support or all blocker resolutions. Owner reports, PR descriptions and promotional comments do not independently validate this workflow. [Source](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/issues) [Source](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/README.md)
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** MIT project-owned code/assets; engine integrations and other dependencies retain their notices. No inference model is bundled. The chosen AI client/model has separate terms and may send map inputs/results to its provider. Local compute plus any chosen external model or service; provider access and charges are separate. [Source](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/docs/mcp/setup.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Layout files do not supply your game’s gameplay. Browser editing and offline MCP writers must not modify the same workspace concurrently. The MCP server itself makes no external requests, but an external AI client may do so.

### How it uses AI

A room-based 2D level editor exposes inspection, Lua generation, dry-run edits, previews and JSON export through a local MCP server so an AI agent can help build editable game layouts. [Source](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/docs/mcp/setup.md)

### Introduction

A room-based 2D level editor exposes inspection, Lua generation, dry-run edits, previews and JSON export through a local MCP server so an AI agent can help build editable game layouts. [Source 1](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/docs/mcp/setup.md)

### What it is good for

Drafting connected rooms, autotiled maps and minimaps for later integration with Unity, Godot, Unreal or SDL projects. [Source 1](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/docs/mcp/setup.md)

The README provides editing GIFs and a six-room sample world. These demonstrate editor operations, not independently validated AI level-design quality. [Source 1](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/docs/mcp/setup.md)

### Install

Use a release launcher with ASP.NET Core Runtime 10, or build with Node 24+, .NET SDK 10 and Git. For agent control, install the CLI and configure a compatible MCP client to launch a version-pinned server against an existing workspace. [Source 1](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/docs/mcp/setup.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Use a release launcher with ASP.NET Core Runtime 10, or build with Node 24+, .NET SDK 10 and Git. For agent control, install the CLI and configure a compatible MCP client to launch a version-pinned server against an existing workspace.

```sh
npm install --global metroidvania-studio
```


```sh
metroidvania-studio mcp --workspace workspace
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/LBarimi/metroidvania-studio/blob/main/docs/mcp/setup.md)

1. Open the sample world and inspect its room connections before making changes.
2. Have the AI client inspect capabilities and the current revision, propose a dry-run batch or Lua edit, and review the layout.
3. Apply the reviewed revision, preview and validate it, then export the room JSON and integrate the relevant engine package.

First complete guide in this library, including completion of any earlier screened entry; not a claim of a launch today.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Turn selected passages from podcasts, voice notes and recorded discussions into captioned vertical, square or widescreen clips without uploading the recording for transcription.

Evidence checked: 2026-10-30. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The README links a hosted editor and an editor screenshot. Its 10–45 seconds per two-minute transcription claim is an author report, not our benchmark or an independent quality test. [Source](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/blob/main/README.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Use the hosted editor without installation, or obtain the official source, install its npm dependencies and run the demo at http://localhost:4340/demo/. Build the embeddable distribution separately with npm run build. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/blob/main/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** The latest-release query returned no records. The latest-updated query returned 0 issue/pull-request records, with a maximum of 10. This snapshot and the reviewed setup/limitations do not establish sustained support or all blocker resolutions. [Source](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/releases) [Source](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/issues)
- **Independent use · unknown:** No substantive independent first-hand use was established in the reviewed primary documentation and bounded issue activity. Owner reports, PR descriptions and promotional comments do not independently validate this workflow. [Source](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/issues) [Source](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/blob/main/README.md)
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** Whisper tiny multilingual is downloaded from Hugging Face. App MIT licensing does not replace model, font, icon, stock-media or optional service terms. MIT application; local computation. The hosted high-resolution route uses rewarded ads, and optional media providers have separate terms. [Source](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/blob/main/README.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. The README’s broad no-upload claim applies to core local processing; model downloads, optional stock searches and ads use networks. Long files and noisy speech can still produce mistakes. Hosted 1080p/1440p exports require rewarded ads; self-hosted copies without an ad unit do not. Minimum RAM/VRAM and total working storage are not stated.

### How it uses AI

A browser audiogram editor uses multilingual Whisper through Transformers.js to transcribe and word-time recordings, then combines editable captions with designed video layouts. [Source](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

A browser audiogram editor uses multilingual Whisper through Transformers.js to transcribe and word-time recordings, then combines editable captions with designed video layouts. [Source 1](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Turn selected passages from podcasts, voice notes and recorded discussions into captioned vertical, square or widescreen clips without uploading the recording for transcription. [Source 1](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

The README links a hosted editor and an editor screenshot. Its 10–45 seconds per two-minute transcription claim is an author report, not our benchmark or an independent quality test. [Source 1](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Use the hosted editor without installation, or obtain the official source, install its npm dependencies and run the demo at http://localhost:4340/demo/. Build the embeddable distribution separately with npm run build. [Source 1](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Use the hosted editor without installation, or obtain the official source, install its npm dependencies and run the demo at http://localhost:4340/demo/. Build the embeddable distribution separately with npm run build.

```sh
git clone https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite
```


```sh
cd soundbite
```


```sh
npm install
```


```sh
npm run demo
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/blob/main/README.md)

1. Open a recording and select the passage to transcribe first; inspect language and every caption.
2. Trim, split or reorder passages, correct word timing, choose a layout and add your own artwork.
3. Export a short MP4 and inspect caption timing, joins and audio before exporting the complete clip.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** First use downloads roughly 44–66 MB of speech weights and 27 MB of ONNX WASM runtime. No dedicated GPU is required by the documented fallback; memory and disk minima are unspecified.
- **Software:** A current browser with WebAssembly; WebGPU accelerates the encoder when available. WebCodecs accelerates export when supported. Node.js/npm are needed for the source route; a minimum Node version is not stated.
- **Platforms:** Browser workflow; no independently checked OS/browser compatibility matrix. CPU/WASM fallback is documented, and browser feature support matters more than assuming support from the interface.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/blob/main/README.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/blob/main/NOTICE)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** NOTICE identifies Whisper as MIT, downloaded as ONNX separately; runtime/fonts/drawings/stock media retain their stated MIT, Apache, OFL, CC or provider terms. Downloaded third-party assets are not all covered by the app’s MIT grant.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. Whisper tiny multilingual is downloaded from Hugging Face. App MIT licensing does not replace model, font, icon, stock-media or optional service terms.
- **Cost:** MIT application; local computation. The hosted high-resolution route uses rewarded ads, and optional media providers have separate terms.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/blob/main/README.md)

### Limitations

The README’s broad no-upload claim applies to core local processing; model downloads, optional stock searches and ads use networks. Long files and noisy speech can still produce mistakes. Hosted 1080p/1440p exports require rewarded ads; self-hosted copies without an ad unit do not. Minimum RAM/VRAM and total working storage are not stated. [Source 1](https://github.com/drkxbt/soundbite/blob/main/README.md)

### How it uses AI

### Introduction

A headless build123d/OpenCascade service renders exact CAD scripts and optionally requests text-to-CAD generation from Zoo, returning geometry files and machine-readable warnings. [Source 1](https://github.com/rojosinalma/cad-render/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Agent-assisted parametric part generation, inspecting CAD outputs and converting STEP/STL/3MF files in an automated creative fabrication pipeline. [Source 1](https://github.com/rojosinalma/cad-render/blob/main/README.md)

The README includes a service demo image and example bracket/boss commands. Exact-kernel geometry and a successful file conversion do not prove a part is fit for printing or manufacture. [Source 1](https://github.com/rojosinalma/cad-render/blob/main/README.md)

Obtain the official source, copy the example environment file, configure Zoo access only for text-to-CAD, and start the Docker Compose service. Check the health route. Pin a release with CAD_IMAGE_TAG for repeatability. [Source 1](https://github.com/rojosinalma/cad-render/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.


### How it uses AI

A CLI and built-in MCP server let an AI coding agent create and edit generative-art sketches, then render, sweep parameters and export images, HTML or video. [Source](https://github.com/genart-dev/cli/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

A CLI and built-in MCP server let an AI coding agent create and edit generative-art sketches, then render, sweep parameters and export images, HTML or video. [Source 1](https://github.com/genart-dev/cli/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Reproducible p5, Canvas2D, Three.js, GLSL and SVG sketch exploration with saved seeds, parameter sweeps and contact-sheet montages. [Source 1](https://github.com/genart-dev/cli/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

The README supplies a complete hybrid agent/CLI example. It is a workflow example rather than a viewed independent creative-output demonstration. [Source 1](https://github.com/genart-dev/cli/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Install the official npm package globally. Diagnose the client configuration and browser/render dependencies before connecting an agent. Preview agent configuration changes with the documented dry-run option. [Source 1](https://github.com/genart-dev/cli/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Install the official npm package globally. Diagnose the client configuration and browser/render dependencies before connecting an agent. Preview agent configuration changes with the documented dry-run option.

```sh
npm install -g @genart-dev/cli
```


```sh
genart agent doctor
```


```sh
genart agent install vscode --dry-run
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/genart-dev/cli/blob/main/README.md)

1. Create or import a sketch, then connect a separately configured agent for source edits.
2. Validate and render one seed; vary parameters or seeds in a small batch and retain the manifest.
3. Export HTML or images; use ffmpeg for video and sharp for a montage, then inspect the result.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/genart-dev/cli/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Node.js/npm, Chrome/Chromium for rendering, ffmpeg on PATH for video, optional sharp for montage. Minimum Node version is not stated in this README.
- **Platforms:** Command-line/browser-rendering workflow with macOS client paths illustrated. A complete tested OS matrix is not documented.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/genart-dev/cli/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/genart-dev/cli/blob/main/README.md)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** The CLI includes no generative model; agent intelligence comes from the user’s chosen model/client. Renderer, browser and optional package licenses remain separate.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. The CLI includes no generative model; agent intelligence comes from the user’s chosen model/client. Renderer, browser and optional package licenses remain separate.
- **Cost:** MIT CLI; local rendering plus the chosen agent/model costs.

Most batch/render operations are deterministic tools. AI relevance is the documented MCP sketch-authoring integration. Live or expensive sketches can hit rendering timeouts; seed reproducibility does not guarantee artistic quality. [Source 1](https://github.com/genart-dev/cli/blob/main/README.md)

A documented thin-wall failure illustrates why rendered appearance is not sufficient fit/print evidence. [Source](https://github.com/KD5RYN/b123d-print/blob/main/README.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Install uv and configure the documented b123d-print-mcp command in a compatible client, with a chosen parts workspace. Owner reports, PR descriptions and promotional comments do not independently validate this workflow. [Source](https://github.com/KD5RYN/b123d-print/issues) [Source](https://github.com/KD5RYN/b123d-print/blob/main/README.md)

The chosen assistant/model is external and may receive measurements/images. MIT tooling, local compute, separate AI-client costs and any printer/material costs. Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. FDM parts only, not general manufacturing certification. Current 0.x part-script conventions may change. Geometry checks and approximate material estimates cannot guarantee a safe load-bearing part or a successful physical print.

### How it uses AI

An MCP/CLI layer lets an AI assistant write build123d part scripts, inspect multiview renders and run FDM-oriented geometry checks before exporting a slicer file. [Source](https://github.com/KD5RYN/b123d-print/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

An MCP/CLI layer lets an AI assistant write build123d part scripts, inspect multiview renders and run FDM-oriented geometry checks before exporting a slicer file. [Source 1](https://github.com/KD5RYN/b123d-print/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Measured brackets, enclosures, spacers and simple remixes with named dimensions and small physical fit coupons. [Source 1](https://github.com/KD5RYN/b123d-print/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

The author shows printed enclosure examples and a bracket-remix preview that is explicitly not printed yet. A documented thin-wall failure illustrates why rendered appearance is not sufficient fit/print evidence. [Source 1](https://github.com/KD5RYN/b123d-print/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Install uv and configure the documented b123d-print-mcp command in a compatible client, with a chosen parts workspace. Restart the client, confirm tool availability and set printer/material/calibration parameters. [Source 1](https://github.com/KD5RYN/b123d-print/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Install uv and configure the documented b123d-print-mcp command in a compatible client, with a chosen parts workspace. Restart the client, confirm tool availability and set printer/material/calibration parameters.

```sh
uvx --from b123d-print b123d-print-mcp --workspace /path/to/parts
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/KD5RYN/b123d-print/blob/main/README.md)

1. Specify a small measured part with named dimensions and printer/material context.
2. Review generated code, six views, section cuts and print checks; adjust dimensions deliberately.
3. Export a small fit coupon and inspect it physically before committing to the full part and slicer settings.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/KD5RYN/b123d-print/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** First use downloads a few hundred MB; minimum CPU, RAM, VRAM and full disk space are not documented. Physical calibration needs the user’s actual FDM printer/material.
- **Software:** uv-managed Python/package dependencies, build123d/OpenCascade and an MCP client that passes images to the selected model. A slicer/printer is separate from digital authoring.
- **Platforms:** CI covers macOS, Ubuntu and Windows according to the README. Native Windows lacks an OS sandbox; Docker/WSL2 is the stronger isolation route. SSH server mode is macOS/Linux only.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/KD5RYN/b123d-print/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/KD5RYN/b123d-print/blob/main/README.md)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** MIT wrapper; CAD kernels, part libraries and printer data retain their own terms. The chosen assistant/model is external and may receive measurements/images.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT wrapper; CAD kernels, part libraries and printer data retain their own terms. The chosen assistant/model is external and may receive measurements/images.
- **Cost:** MIT tooling, local compute, separate AI-client costs and any printer/material costs.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/KD5RYN/b123d-print/blob/main/README.md)

### Limitations

FDM parts only, not general manufacturing certification. Current 0.x part-script conventions may change. Geometry checks and approximate material estimates cannot guarantee a safe load-bearing part or a successful physical print. [Source 1](https://github.com/KD5RYN/b123d-print/blob/main/README.md)

```sh
bash skills/motionkit/scripts/new-project.sh ~/my-reel
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/immousavi/motionkit/blob/main/README.md)

1. Write the brief and storyboard, then edit cue timing, text and style tokens.
2. Ask the agent to implement scene functions; render contact sheets/stills and review actual transitions.
3. Regenerate the score after cue changes, render a short clip, inspect picture/sound and only then build the final MP4.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/immousavi/motionkit/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Node 18+, Chrome/Chromium or Playwright browser, ffmpeg and a compatible coding agent. Software WebGL is documented, so a dedicated GPU is not mandatory.
- **Platforms:** Author-verified Apple M1/macOS and software SwiftShader. Headless Linux Vulkan is documented; the supplied Ubuntu workflow being ready to enable is not a completed CI run or a Windows verification.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/immousavi/motionkit/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/immousavi/motionkit/blob/main/README.md)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** MIT engine/skill; upstream kinetic-reel attribution and OFL Vazirmatn/JetBrains Mono fonts are separate. Chosen agents/models and any imported assets retain their own terms.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT engine/skill; upstream kinetic-reel attribution and OFL Vazirmatn/JetBrains Mono fonts are separate. Chosen agents/models and any imported assets retain their own terms.
- **Cost:** MIT tooling, local rendering and the selected agent/model costs.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/immousavi/motionkit/blob/main/README.md)

### Limitations

Its code synthesizes frames and sound; it is not a general diffusion-video model. Stale score duration can truncate output; RTL text, font rendering and facts need inspection. Minimum RAM, GPU memory and disk are not stated. [Source 1](https://github.com/immousavi/motionkit/blob/main/README.md)

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/Burgstall-labs/ComfyUI-SpEdE/blob/main/README.md)

1. Validate the render fixture and use a permitted scene with capture cameras; select an object and inspect the naming/mask agreement.
2. Extract or remove with the required model permissions; inspect repaired surfaces in other capture views.
3. Compare original/edited 3D and export the PLY. Apply all-around fill before a replacement; current edits do not stack arbitrarily.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/Burgstall-labs/ComfyUI-SpEdE/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** 64 GB RAM recommended; only NVIDIA 32 GB VRAM (RTX 5090) tested. Product weights about 27 GB, replacement adds 3.3 GB, optional orbit adds about 43 GB; all-around output roughly 1.4–2.1 GB/run. Smaller GPUs remain untested.
- **Software:** ComfyUI 0.37+ with core nodes, author-tested 0.41.0; gsplat 1.5+ matching torch/CUDA and possibly nvcc/C++ compiler. Author environment: Python 3.12.3, torch 2.9.1+cu130, Ubuntu 24.04.5, driver 580/CUDA 13.
- **Platforms:** Only one Ubuntu/RTX 5090 environment was tested by the author. Windows instructions are untested starting points; Mac inference support is not established.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/Burgstall-labs/ComfyUI-SpEdE/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/Burgstall-labs/ComfyUI-SpEdE/blob/main/README.md)

- **Code:** AGPL-3.0
- **Weights:** AGPL application grant; SAM, Qwen-Image 2.1 research/non-commercial weights, MoGe, TripoSplat/DINOv3 and scene datasets have separate terms. Open weights does not give unrestricted output rights.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows AGPL-3.0. AGPL application grant; SAM, Qwen-Image 2.1 research/non-commercial weights, MoGe, TripoSplat/DINOv3 and scene datasets have separate terms. Open weights does not give unrestricted output rights.
- **Cost:** AGPL app/local GPU compute. Model and scene rights, especially Qwen research terms, constrain use independently.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/Burgstall-labs/ComfyUI-SpEdE/blob/main/README.md)

### Limitations

Repairs support surfaces, not arbitrary hidden-object 3D inpainting. Replacement backsides are invented and work best near ±15 degrees of the edit view; old shadows can remain. Experimental orbit graphs are separate from the documented product path and do not alone justify an Experimental label for that path. [Source 1](https://github.com/Burgstall-labs/ComfyUI-SpEdE/blob/main/README.md)

### Get the tool

- [Repository](https://github.com/Burgstall-labs/ComfyUI-SpEdE)
- [Documentation](https://github.com/Burgstall-labs/ComfyUI-SpEdE/blob/main/README.md)

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/bolajiev/cadgo/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/bolajiev/cadgo/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/bolajiev/cadgo/blob/main/public/openscad/SOURCE-OFFER.txt)

1. Load a part study, change a parameter, inspect the actual OpenSCAD source and compile one manual edit.
2. Request a small part and inspect the generated source, compile log and any detached-solid repair.
3. Export STL/SCAD for the part studio, or visit /mechanism for articulated assemblies and its distinct STL/GLB/plate workflow; inspect dimensions and geometry before fabrication.

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. - **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Next.js 16/React 19, Node/npm, browser graphics and an approximately 9 MB first-load OpenSCAD WASM module. The separate mechanism lab also uses Manifold. Live AI uses the configured OpenCode Go/OpenAI-compatible endpoint; a minimum Node version is not stated.
- **Platforms:** Browser/local Next.js workflow; no native OS or mobile compatibility matrix is independently verified.

- **Weights:** The current complete GPL-3.0 application grant supersedes the earlier MIT snapshot in this observation. A native Rust renderer and MCP interface let AI agents write Reel Script, inspect lint/contact sheets and render motion graphics without a browser renderer. [Source](https://github.com/agentfxhq/agentfx/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

A native Rust renderer and MCP interface let AI agents write Reel Script, inspect lint/contact sheets and render motion graphics without a browser renderer. [Source 1](https://github.com/agentfxhq/agentfx/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Scripted kinetic type, diagrams, fluid/ink effects, audio-reactive graphics and repeatable multi-format social-video output. [Source 1](https://github.com/agentfxhq/agentfx/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

The README links effect previews, a trailer and example scripts. Deterministic frames establish repeatability of the script, not a universal creative-quality approval. [Source 1](https://github.com/agentfxhq/agentfx/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Prepare Rust stable 1.85+ and FFmpeg on the documented Linux route. Obtain the source and build in place, or review the installer. Register the optional Python standard-library MCP server after the binaries are available. [Source 1](https://github.com/agentfxhq/agentfx/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Prepare Rust stable 1.85+ and FFmpeg on the documented Linux route. Obtain the source and build in place, or review the installer. Register the optional Python standard-library MCP server after the binaries are available.

```sh
git clone https://github.com/agentfxhq/agentfx
```


```sh
cd agentfx
```


```sh
cargo build --release
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/agentfxhq/agentfx/blob/main/README.md)

1. Ask the agent to create a short Reel Script using the documented format/catalog.
2. Lint all target aspect ratios and inspect single frames/contact sheets.
3. Render one short clip, listen/check captions and refine before final multi-format export.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/agentfxhq/agentfx/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Rust 1.85+, FFmpeg; optional Vulkan driver for GPU effects, Python for MCP and separately installed Piper for local TTS.
- **Platforms:** Tested Linux x86_64. macOS/Windows are explicitly experimental and untested. CPU rendering is available when Vulkan is unavailable.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/agentfxhq/agentfx/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/agentfxhq/agentfx/blob/main/README.md)

- **Code:** Apache-2.0
- **Weights:** Apache-2.0 application with NOTICE; vendored code/font licenses include MPL/MIT/Apache/OFL. Optional TTS models and agent provider terms remain separate.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows Apache-2.0. Apache-2.0 application with NOTICE; vendored code/font licenses include MPL/MIT/Apache/OFL. Optional TTS models and agent provider terms remain separate.
- **Cost:** Apache renderer/local compute, optional voice-model resources and chosen AI agent costs.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/agentfxhq/agentfx/blob/main/README.md)

### Limitations

Current 0.1.0 format/CLI may change. It offers 2.5D rather than true mesh 3D, and no timeline GUI. The optional edge TTS uses an undocumented online service; Piper is the default local route. Music must be supplied rather than composed by the renderer. [Source 1](https://github.com/agentfxhq/agentfx/blob/main/README.md)

### Get the tool

- [Repository](https://github.com/agentfxhq/agentfx)
- [Documentation](https://github.com/agentfxhq/agentfx/blob/main/README.md)

## Retypo

First complete guide in this library, including completion of any earlier screened entry; not a claim of a launch today.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Revising text on permitted posters, mockups, screenshots and social graphics without rebuilding every element manually.

Evidence checked: 2026-10-30. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The README links a hosted demo/API and reports CPU/Render benchmarks. Its exact-reconstruction and recall claims are author reports, not independently validated guarantees. [Source](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/blob/main/README.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Prepare Node 18+ and Python 3.10/3.11. Obtain the source, install backend dependencies in a virtual environment and start the backend; install/start the Vite frontend in another terminal. Optional Docker/Render/Vercel deployment is separate. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/blob/main/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** The latest-release query returned no records. The latest-updated query returned 0 issue/pull-request records, with a maximum of 10. This snapshot and the reviewed setup/limitations do not establish sustained support or all blocker resolutions. [Source](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/releases) [Source](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/issues)
- **Independent use · unknown:** No substantive independent first-hand use was established in the reviewed primary documentation and bounded issue activity. Owner reports, PR descriptions and promotional comments do not independently validate this workflow. [Source](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/issues) [Source](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/blob/main/README.md)
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** MIT code; Paddle/Google OCR, weights, Google fonts and deployment services keep separate terms. Local OCR and Google Cloud Vision have different privacy/cost implications. MIT app and local OCR compute, or separately billed Google Vision/hosting services. [Source](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/blob/main/README.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. The neural component is OCR, not every inpainting step. A small hosted-memory benchmark is not a universal RAM ceiling, and font matching/background repair can be wrong. The README’s memory, no-leak and pixel-preservation claims need reproduction; JPEG export is not bitwise preservation.

### How it uses AI

A raster-text editor uses PaddleOCR or Google Vision to detect text and turn it into editable Fabric.js layers, with conventional background-patch reconstruction and typography estimates. [Source](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

A raster-text editor uses PaddleOCR or Google Vision to detect text and turn it into editable Fabric.js layers, with conventional background-patch reconstruction and typography estimates. [Source 1](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Revising text on permitted posters, mockups, screenshots and social graphics without rebuilding every element manually. [Source 1](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

The README links a hosted demo/API and reports CPU/Render benchmarks. Its exact-reconstruction and recall claims are author reports, not independently validated guarantees. [Source 1](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Prepare Node 18+ and Python 3.10/3.11. Obtain the source, install backend dependencies in a virtual environment and start the backend; install/start the Vite frontend in another terminal. Optional Docker/Render/Vercel deployment is separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Prepare Node 18+ and Python 3.10/3.11. Obtain the source, install backend dependencies in a virtual environment and start the backend; install/start the Vite frontend in another terminal. Optional Docker/Render/Vercel deployment is separate.

```sh
python3.11 -m venv venv
```


```sh
pip install -r backend/requirements.txt
```


```sh
python run_server.py
```


```sh
cd frontend && npm install && npm run dev
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/blob/main/README.md)

1. Load a small permitted image and inspect OCR region boundaries.
2. Correct text/font guesses and patch overlays, keeping a copy of the original.
3. Export a project JSON and a raster preview, comparing non-text areas and typography at final resolution.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Author reports about 74.5 MB baseline/220 MB inference on a 512 MB constrained service. This is not a universal minimum or ceiling; system/VRAM/storage requirements are otherwise unspecified.
- **Software:** Python 3.10 or 3.11, Node 18+/npm, FastAPI and the listed OCR/OpenCV dependencies. Backend loopback:8000 and frontend:5173; optional Google Vision credentials are separate.
- **Platforms:** Source instructions show POSIX and Windows environment activation. No complete independently tested native OS/browser matrix is given.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/blob/main/README.md)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** MIT code; Paddle/Google OCR, weights, Google fonts and deployment services keep separate terms. Local OCR and Google Cloud Vision have different privacy/cost implications.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT code; Paddle/Google OCR, weights, Google fonts and deployment services keep separate terms. Local OCR and Google Cloud Vision have different privacy/cost implications.
- **Cost:** MIT app and local OCR compute, or separately billed Google Vision/hosting services.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/blob/main/README.md)

### Limitations

The neural component is OCR, not every inpainting step. A small hosted-memory benchmark is not a universal RAM ceiling, and font matching/background repair can be wrong. The README’s memory, no-leak and pixel-preservation claims need reproduction; JPEG export is not bitwise preservation. [Source 1](https://github.com/PannagaJA/Retypo/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Begin with the standalone mv-studio using Node 22+, FFmpeg/ffprobe and the official source. Disable models to inspect the canvas first; configure private provider keys only for selected generation.
```sh
git clone https://github.com/oniszczukthommy-wq/Osmanthus.git
```


```sh
cd Osmanthus/mv-studio
```


```sh
MV_DISABLE_MODELS=1 npm start
```

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/oniszczukthommy-wq/Osmanthus/blob/main/README.md)

1. Import your own permitted media into the standalone canvas at loopback:8792.
2. Configure one optional provider and review lyrics/shot plans before generation.
3. Listen to candidate music, edit the MV and inspect actual export; do not assume the full inherited music environment exists.
4. The new homepage inspiration control asks DeepSeek for editable theme/arrangement suggestions without starting a song. Review real-time lyric alignment separately; first synchronization can take preparation time.

- **Platforms:** Local-browser prototype with POSIX startup examples. A fully tested native Windows/Mac/Linux setup matrix is not documented.

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/DouglasGDev/audio-pulse/blob/main/README.md)

1. Begin with a permitted local WAV or built-in reference sound and use bypass to understand the chain.

### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/DouglasGDev/audio-pulse/blob/main/README.md)

- **Software:** Node 20.19+ or 22.12+, npm/Rust stable. Windows needs C++ Build Tools/SDK/WebView2; macOS needs Xcode tools; Linux needs the listed WebKit/ALSA/build libraries.
- **Platforms:** Windows 10/11 is the reference platform. macOS 11+ and Linux build/routing routes are documented, with some Windows-only volume/battery features; they are not independently validated here.
