A Claude Code-driven editor compares a software recording with project documentation, drafts a cited storyboard, renders FFmpeg proxies and exports platform-specific short videos through explicit approval gates. [Source 1](https://github.com/sinsankio/ave/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Turning a permitted software-demo recording into reviewed LinkedIn, Reels and Facebook edits with editable timing and captions. [Source 1](https://github.com/sinsankio/ave/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

The README documents the pipeline, EDL structure and quality checks. A completed independently assessed demo export was not established in this review; automated checks do not prove compelling pacing or accurate claims. [Source 1](https://github.com/sinsankio/ave/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Obtain the source, prepare Python 3.13+, uv, FFmpeg 6+/FFprobe and an authenticated Claude Code client. Install the optional OCR extra if needed, inspect doctor output and replace the author’s default brand/writing-skill settings before starting. [Source 1](https://github.com/sinsankio/ave/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Obtain the source, prepare Python 3.13+, uv, FFmpeg 6+/FFprobe and an authenticated Claude Code client. Install the optional OCR extra if needed, inspect doctor output and replace the author’s default brand/writing-skill settings before starting.

```sh
uv sync --extra ocr
```


```sh
uv run ave doctor
```


```sh
uv run ave init --name "My Project" --video demo.mp4 --docs "docs/*.md" --platform linkedin --platform reels
```


```sh
uv run ave run
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/sinsankio/ave/blob/main/README.md)

1. Use a recording and documentation you are permitted to send to the selected agent, and edit project.yaml for your identity and writing workflow.
2. Review docs-versus-video conflicts, storyboard and copy, then approve or revise the proposed plan.
3. Inspect proxies, caption/safe-zone checks and change requests before approving final platform renders.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/sinsankio/ave/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Python 3.13+, uv, FFmpeg/FFprobe 6+, Claude Code login/subscription; OCR uses RapidOCR/ONNX Runtime with first-use model downloads. NVIDIA NVENC is optional, with libx264 CPU fallback.
- **Platforms:** The example FFmpeg install uses an apt-based Linux path. A completed Windows/macOS support matrix is not documented; the language/tool prerequisites alone do not prove those platforms.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/sinsankio/ave/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/sinsankio/ave/blob/main/README.md)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** MIT application. Claude Code/model subscription, optional LangSmith, OCR models, fonts, recordings and brand assets have separate terms and privacy/cost behavior. API backend credentials may pass doctor, but the current api backend refuses to run agents.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT application. Claude Code/model subscription, optional LangSmith, OCR models, fonts, recordings and brand assets have separate terms and privacy/cost behavior. API backend credentials may pass doctor, but the current api backend refuses to run agents.
- **Cost:** The current agent pipeline requires the user’s Claude Code account/subscription plus local processing; optional LangSmith has separate service terms. The reported API-equivalent token estimate is not a per-video subscription charge.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/sinsankio/ave/blob/main/README.md)

### Limitations

This edits an existing recording; it does not install or operate the demonstrated app and does not publish to social accounts. Defaults reference the author’s own brand and writing skill. Optional LangSmith can send frames/thumbnails, and API-equivalent token estimates are not subscription invoices. [Source 1](https://github.com/sinsankio/ave/blob/main/README.md)

### Get the tool

- [Repository](https://github.com/sinsankio/ave)
- [Documentation](https://github.com/sinsankio/ave/blob/main/README.md)

## OpenDub

Video, animation & film · Audio, music & voice · Accessible media & assistive creation · AI visual dubbing and translated performance

First complete guide in this library; v1.0.5 was published October 27, not a launch today.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

The app grant and creative workflow are supported; independent reliability and complete dependency clearance remain unresolved.

Scope: Timed, editable translation and neural voice dubbing of authorized short videos.

Evidence checked: 2026-10-30. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** Reviewed the complete current AGPL v3 text, byte-for-byte identical to a preserved complete standard AGPL review, and read the complete project NOTICE applying version 3-or-later. The NOTICE separately discloses bundled FFmpeg/PyAV GPL components and source offers, permissive vendor components and OFL fonts. App-code openness does not cover model weights, voice rights or API services; OmniVoice weights are non-commercial and optional engines have their own terms. [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/NOTICE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The author supplies a bilingual demo and timing claims; these are not an independent accuracy or voice-fidelity evaluation. [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://opendub.app/)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Installer/source launch routes and first-use steps are documented; independent reproduction has not been established. [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/engines/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** Five recent release records include app v1.0.5 on October 27 and extension releases; the queried issue list was empty. These bounded observations do not establish sustained support or blocker resolution. [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/releases) [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/issues)
- **Independent use · unknown:** Independent first-hand creative use was not established from the primary project, release and issue evidence reviewed. [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/issues)
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** AGPL-3.0-or-later application; OmniVoice code is Apache-2.0 but its weights are CC-BY-NC. Chatterbox/CosyVoice/VoxCPM terms differ, and IndexTTS-2 permission remains unconfirmed. Hosted Higgs/ElevenLabs services and voice/input rights are separate. The default Higgs pipeline sends audio/text/voice references to the service even though it keeps the full video local. Free/local routes must be selected and checked separately. Browser jobs are limited to five minutes. VoxCPM2 and IndexTTS-2 paths are explicitly untested; no lip-sync animation is established. Translation, timing and voice fidelity need human review. [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/NOTICE) [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/engines/README.md)

Source review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run the app. The default Higgs pipeline sends audio/text/voice references to the service even though it keeps the full video local. Free/local routes must be selected and checked separately. Browser jobs are limited to five minutes. VoxCPM2 and IndexTTS-2 paths are explicitly untested; no lip-sync animation is established. Translation, timing and voice fidelity need human review.

### How it uses AI

Neural speech recognition, translation and voice cloning generate a timed foreign-language dub with editable lines and subtitle export. [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/engines/README.md)

### Introduction

Neural speech recognition, translation and voice cloning generate a timed foreign-language dub with editable lines and subtitle export. [Source 1](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/engines/README.md)

### What it is good for

Preparing reviewable multilingual dubs of authorized creator videos while retaining music, effects, editable lines and subtitle files. [Source 1](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/engines/README.md)

### Demo & examples

The publisher supplies an English-to-Simplified-Chinese demo and reports roughly four minutes for a 74-second clip on an M1 Max using rate-limited Higgs calls. These are author observations, not independently reproduced benchmarks. The current official site also offers a free stock-voice browser route on supported desktop/Android browsers; it does not clone the speaker, downloading a dub requires a free sign-in, and iPhone support remains a preview. [Source 1](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/engines/README.md) [Source 3](https://opendub.app/?phonevoice=1)

### Install

Use the Windows or Apple Silicon full installer, which bundles Python/FFmpeg and packages, or obtain the source with Python 3.12 and FFmpeg built with libass/rubberband. The first local voice choice downloads its model; configure the selected local engine or an authorized provider account. [Source 1](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/engines/README.md)

1. Download the matching official release, or prepare the documented source dependencies.
2. For source use, run the repository launcher to create its environment; select and review a voice engine and any provider configuration.

```sh
git clone https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub opendub
```


```sh
cd opendub
```


```sh
./run.sh
```

### First project

Begin with a short authorized clip and compare original and dub before a longer project. [Source 1](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/engines/README.md)

1. Choose spoken/target languages, replace-voice or voice-over mode, and the selected local or hosted engine.
2. Run a short dub, review translated lines and timing, then drag or edit one line and re-dub it.
3. Compare both versions and export MP4, WAV and source/target SRT files.
### Hardware & software

The documented paths have different runtimes and privacy/cost characteristics. [Source 1](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/engines/README.md)

- **Hardware:** The first OmniVoice download is about 3.3 GB; Demucs and Whisper small source-path downloads are about 80 MB and 480 MB. System RAM, universal VRAM and total disk minima are not documented. VoxCPM2’s separate untested route specifies NVIDIA 8 GB+.
- **Software:** Source: Python 3.12, FFmpeg with libass/rubberband and requirements including av<19. Voice engines use isolated environments because dependency pins conflict. Browser route needs WebCodecs.
- **Platforms:** Official full installers target Windows and Apple Silicon macOS. Browser/extension routes are documented for capable browsers. A validated Linux or Intel-Mac installer is not established.

### License, model weights & costs

Complete application grant and NOTICE reviewed; each bundled runtime, model and service keeps its own terms. [Source 1](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/NOTICE) [Source 3](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source 4](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/engines/README.md) [Source 5](https://opendub.app/licences) [Source 6](https://opendub.app/?phonevoice=1)

- **Code:** AGPL-3.0-or-later
- **Weights:** AGPL-3.0-or-later application; OmniVoice code is Apache-2.0 but its weights are CC-BY-NC. Chatterbox/CosyVoice/VoxCPM terms differ, and IndexTTS-2 permission remains unconfirmed. Hosted Higgs/ElevenLabs services and voice/input rights are separate. The publisher separately identifies GPL FFmpeg/x264/x265 components in particular desktop installers and offers their corresponding source; these do not become AGPL application code.
- **Commercial:** AGPL app-code permissions do not make non-commercial OmniVoice weights or unconfirmed engines suitable for commercial outputs. Check the chosen engine, service and voice rights.
- **Cost:** Local compute/model downloads for local engines, or separately charged provider calls. The publisher reports about 70 Higgs calls for its demonstration; no universal per-video price is documented. The official stock-voice browser route is free within its documented limits; it differs from the local cloned voice and paid BYOK routes.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented neural dubbing/editing workflow and reviewed software grant. Publisher demos/tests do not establish reliable independent quality. [Source 1](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/engines/README.md)

### Limitations

The default Higgs pipeline sends audio/text/voice references to the service even though it keeps the full video local. Free/local routes must be selected and checked separately. Browser jobs are limited to five minutes. VoxCPM2 and IndexTTS-2 paths are explicitly untested; no lip-sync animation is established. Translation, timing and voice fidelity need human review. [Source 1](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/engines/README.md)

### Get the tool

- [Repository](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub)
- [Documentation](https://gitlab.com/open-dubapp/OpenDub/-/blob/main/README.md)
- [Website](https://opendub.app/)
- [Demo](https://opendub.app/?phonevoice=1)

## OpenSlides

Creative publishing & presentation · AI kinetic typography and animated lettering · Video, animation & film · Browser tools & web media · Creative learning & authoring

First detailed guide in this library; not a claim that the project launched today.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Complete software grants and a concrete AI-assisted deck workflow are supported; independent output and setup reliability remain unverified.

Scope: Agent-assisted animated deck authoring, narration and reviewed video/static exports.

Evidence checked: 2026-10-30. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** Read the entire MIT grant and alternative Apache-2.0 text, including attribution, patent/redistribution conditions and warranty disclaimers; the README explicitly applies MIT OR Apache-2.0 and separates OFL fonts. [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/LICENSE-MIT) [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/LICENSE-APACHE) [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/README.md)
- **Creative results · documented:** The primary site supplies a narrated animated keynote example; selected author output does not establish general deck quality. [Source](https://openslides.dev/)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Hosted studio and npm source launch are documented; the separate updater MCP has an explicit unpublished dependency blocker. [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** Five latest commits were retrieved, alongside empty issue and release lists. This bounded snapshot does not establish support durability or blocker resolution. [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/commits/main) [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/issues) [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/releases)
- **Independent use · unknown:** Independent first-hand use was not established from the primary site, repository and issue evidence. [Source](https://openslides.dev/) [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/issues)
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** No universal bundled LLM. Bring a separately licensed local model or a cloud account/key/credits; local/browser/cloud voice routes differ. Fonts have SIL OFL 1.1 terms, distinct from MIT OR Apache-2.0 code. Cloud AI and cloud voices send the relevant content to the chosen provider; local editing does not make every optional model private. The source-tree PowerPoint-updater MCP crate depends on an unpublished support crate and cannot yet build independently. Studio integrations ship separately. Static PPTX export does not preserve all animated/interactive behavior, and author layout-fit measurements are not independent guarantees. [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://openslides.dev/)

Documentation/source review only; not installed or run. Cloud AI and cloud voices send the relevant content to the chosen provider; local editing does not make every optional model private. The source-tree PowerPoint-updater MCP crate depends on an unpublished support crate and cannot yet build independently. Studio integrations ship separately. Static PPTX export does not preserve all animated/interactive behavior, and author layout-fit measurements are not independent guarantees.

### How it uses AI

A browser slide studio lets an optional LLM generate and revise editable TSX slide compositions, while local tools provide animation, narration, multilingual video and static deck export. [Source](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://openslides.dev/)

### Introduction

A browser slide studio lets an optional LLM generate and revise editable TSX slide compositions, while local tools provide animation, narration, multilingual video and static deck export. [Source 1](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://openslides.dev/)

### What it is good for

Editable animated teaching decks, design presentations and narrated slide videos with agent-assisted composition and local manual controls. [Source 1](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://openslides.dev/)

### Demo & examples

The primary site presents a 4:18 fictional solar-company keynote with narration and local video rendering. It is an author example; text fit and visual polish were not independently reproduced. [Source 1](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://openslides.dev/)

### Install

Use the official browser studio without an account for manual editing, or obtain the GitLab source and install its npm workspace. The source route serves the Studio at localhost:5320. Configure a model only for AI functions; the separate Rust/WASM updater follows its own build route. [Source 1](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://openslides.dev/)

1. Obtain the official source or open the hosted Studio.
2. Install npm dependencies and start dev:studio; configure optional model/voice access privately.

```sh
git clone https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides.git
```


```sh
cd openslides
```


```sh
npm install
```


```sh
npm run dev:studio
```

### First project

Start from notes or the sample deck; keep generated content editable and review its export. [Source 1](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://openslides.dev/)

1. Review a brief/outline and choose a visual direction; with no AI key the initial outline uses headings and bullets.
2. Use the agent or manual controls to revise a slide; inspect code, overflow, fonts and actual provider costs.
3. Record/generate narration and preview timing. Export a short video or static PDF/PPTX and compare it with the Studio view.
### Hardware & software

Browser Studio and Rust/WASM updater are separate routes with different prerequisites. [Source 1](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://openslides.dev/)

- **Hardware:** Minimum CPU, RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed README. Rendering requirements depend on deck complexity and chosen local models.
- **Software:** Browser for hosted editing; Node/npm for source Studio. Optional openslides-render uses Hyperframes or Playwright. Rust/WASM is part of the separate updater source build; exact minimum versions are not established here.
- **Platforms:** Browser workflow with phone/tablet editing described on the primary site. No independent OS/browser compatibility or mobile local-inference matrix was verified.

### License, model weights & costs

Complete root dual-license grants reviewed; fonts, models and services retain separate terms. [Source 1](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/LICENSE-MIT) [Source 2](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/LICENSE-APACHE) [Source 3](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source 4](https://openslides.dev/)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** No universal bundled LLM. Bring a separately licensed local model or a cloud account/key/credits; local/browser/cloud voice routes differ. Fonts have SIL OFL 1.1 terms, distinct from MIT OR Apache-2.0 code.
- **Commercial:** MIT OR Apache-2.0 permits commercial code use under the selected grant’s conditions; no blanket font/model/media/service rights are implied.
- **Cost:** Manual Studio is described as free without account; optional BYOK models, local compute and paid credits/voices remain separate.

### Why it merits attention

Included for a concrete AI-assisted creative workflow. Author tests and polished demos do not replace independent use. [Source 1](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://openslides.dev/)

### Limitations

Cloud AI and cloud voices send the relevant content to the chosen provider; local editing does not make every optional model private. The source-tree PowerPoint-updater MCP crate depends on an unpublished support crate and cannot yet build independently. Studio integrations ship separately. Static PPTX export does not preserve all animated/interactive behavior, and author layout-fit measurements are not independent guarantees. [Source 1](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://openslides.dev/)

### Get the tool

- [Repository](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides)
- [Documentation](https://gitlab.com/open-slides/openslides/-/blob/main/README.md)
- [Website](https://openslides.dev/)

## Additional open-source AI discoveries

Creative AI relevance and software license screened. Full installation, requirements and quality profiles are pending.

### RDNA2 image/video generation recipes · MIT

Reproducing creator image/video experiments on Ubuntu with an RX 6900 XT rather than assuming a current NVIDIA stack.
Pinned ComfyUI workflows, patches and launch helpers enable Qwen-Image, MOSS-Image and MiniMax H3 inference on a specified older AMD GPU.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. MIT original code/documentation; patches retain GPL/Apache/MIT upstream grants, fetched patientx INT8 code is AGPL, and models/LoRAs/upscalers retain their own terms, including research-only cases. The author’s reference is RX 6900 XT 16 GB, 64 GB RAM, Ubuntu and ROCm 10.2 nightly wheels. Most timing/VRAM observations are single runs and sampling can miss memory spikes. These are API-format workflows with placeholder prompts/input names, not complete ComfyUI UI graphs or general support for every AMD device.

Two portable agent workflows direct Three.js world/asset creation and Blender modeling, rigging, skinning and export, with separate Roblox validation.

### Shiguang image tool · MIT

Batch photo preparation with locally executed cutout and background-replacement steps.
A local Python/Qt image editor uses neural subject segmentation for background removal and ID-photo background replacement alongside conventional image tools.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. MIT app source is actually present as main_qt.py, alongside requirements. It uses PySide6, Pillow and ONNX Runtime, with BiRefNet-Portrait and RMBG-1.4 fallback models. The inspected implementation selects ONNX CPUExecutionProvider, which does not establish speed or a minimum machine specification. Model-weight terms and Qt/runtime redistribution terms are separate and were not fully established here. The documented packaged layout uses a Windows EXE; a working Mac/Linux package is not established. No RAM/VRAM/storage minimum or independently verified cutout accuracy is documented.

### Markilo · Apache-2.0

Agent-assisted identity exploration with editable vector sources and explicit comparison evidence.
A Claude Code brand-design workflow creates original SVG identities, uses deterministic measurements/reference comparison and exports asset kits.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. Apache toolchain with separately licensed fonts and model access. Reference-based similarity covers only collected marks and does not clear a trademark or certify originality. The author’s self-identity demo had no trademark-registry clearance; complete setup and independent design use remain unverified. The current rc.3 documentation discloses weak unattended results: one of seven owner-run trials passed its gate and the blind judge rejected that identity as presentable. Use it for human-reviewed exploration; neither an internal critic nor AUC calibration clears quality, uniqueness or trademark rights.

### Dub Assistant — neural performance mixer · GPL-3.0

Exploring learned macro control for a live dub mix, rather than generating a finished song.
A Max for Live device learns a three-input, 24-output neural mapping for coordinated sends, probabilities and fader changes across eight tracks.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. The complete GPL-3.0 grant includes an or-later application notice. Ableton Live 11/12 with Max for Live is a separate proprietary host; Windows and Intel/Apple Silicon macOS are author-documented. The checked device folder contains the AMXD, Max patch, JavaScript engine and sample data. Documentation is inconsistent: README describes 16 hidden neurons and the guide says 50 epochs, while current JavaScript uses eight hidden tanh units and 550 epochs, with at least eight training rows. Training/import behavior, audio-thread performance and actual mix results need verification. No audio demo, RAM/VRAM minimum or independent performance test was established.

### osc-bridge agent skill · GPL-3.0

Agent-assisted performance control with inspectable OSC routes and hardware-specific parameter mappings.
An AI-agent skill directs an OSC/MIDI bridge to operate synthesizers, DAWs and live-coding environments, including TouchDesigner and Pure Data.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. The complete skill license is GPL-3.0; the current engine instead has a complete AGPL-3.0-or-later grant, despite the skill README saying their grants match. The skill describes an older cloned-catalog route while the current engine can fetch/cache SynthHub drivers; these setup versions need reconciliation. Individual device data retain separate source licenses, and proprietary DAWs, controllers and any model/client or hosted service have separate requirements and costs. A successful UDP send does not acknowledge host action; get_status may report no reply even with a running bridge, ports can collide, and OSC integer/float types and host-side plugins matter. Neither the stated 849-device catalog nor each cross-platform performance path was independently qualified.

## Excluded and unresolved findings

Research notes only; these do not enter the eligible tool library.

- **RAVE** · excluded: Current repository software LICENSE is CC BY-NC 4.0, a non-commercial grant rather than a qualifying open-source software license. [Source](https://github.com/acids-ircam/RAVE/blob/master/LICENSE)
- **nn_tilde** · excluded: Current repository software LICENSE is CC BY-NC 4.0, so its useful neural audio/dataflow integration does not qualify for this open-source software library. [Source](https://github.com/acids-ircam/nn_tilde/blob/master/LICENSE)
- **Axiom Engineer CAD** · excluded: The current README explicitly says implementation has not started and no functional CAD capabilities are claimed. A design specification is not a working creative AI tool. [Source](https://github.com/Krozzeo/AxiomEngineerCAD)
- **video-gen** · excluded: The current M0 commit is documentation only and explicitly has no generation code. [Source](https://github.com/nmp-dsci/video-gen)
- **MangaDock** · excluded: The current stage provides infrastructure/provider configuration only; its README explicitly says the novel/comic generation pipeline is not implemented. [Source](https://github.com/hucong730/MangaDock)
- **bili-ai-whisper** · needs-license-review: A complete MIT root grant was retrieved, but the README also says the project is only for personal learning/technical research. The apparent scope conflict needs clarification before a new eligible listing. [Source](https://github.com/Yaki-Blake/bili-ai-whisper)
- **mITyStudio** · needs-license-review: The primary site describes creative inference, but the repository license endpoint returned 404 and a complete software grant was not established. [Source](https://github.com/janvanwassenhove/mITyStudio)
- **VFXEdu CorridorKey 2.0 workbench** · needs-license-review: The repository returned a custom Corridor Key license (NOASSERTION). Complete qualifying software permission was not established, so metadata and the useful keying description do not earn a new eligible listing. [Source](https://github.com/VFXEdu/VFXEdu-CorridorKey2.0)
- **Stable Audio 3 tilde exporter** · needs-license-review: The complete root file is MIT, but the current README identifies that same file as a Stability AI Community License. Clarification of the software-versus-weight scope is needed before eligibility; platform-specific exports and model access remain separate. [Source](https://github.com/jasper-zheng/stable-audio-3-tilde/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/jasper-zheng/stable-audio-3-tilde/blob/main/LICENSE)
